### Tax and SARS

**[Official] SARS — Turnover Tax**
https://www.sars.gov.za/types-of-tax/turnover-tax/
Supports: micro business qualification, the R2.3 million threshold for years of assessment ending between 1 March 2026 and 28 February 2027, reduced record-keeping.

**[Official] SARS — Budget 2026 Frequently Asked Questions**
https://www.sars.gov.za/about/sars-tax-and-customs-system/budget/budget-2026-frequently-asked-questions/
Supports: turnover tax threshold increase to R2.3 million; turnover tax replacing income tax, VAT, provisional tax, capital gains tax and dividends tax; VAT thresholds of R2.3 million compulsory and R120,000 voluntary from 1 April 2026; SARS interest rate of 10.25% on late payment from 2 March 2026.

**[Official] SARS — What is the new threshold for VAT registration?**
https://www.sars.gov.za/faq/what-is-the-new-threshold-for-vat-registration/
Supports: compulsory VAT registration threshold rose from R1 million to R2.3 million on 1 April 2026.

**[Official] SARS — Tax calendar**
https://www.sars.gov.za/individuals/i-need-help-with-my-tax/calendar/
Supports: IRP6 dates of 31 August, 28 February and 30 September; EMP201 due by the 7th of the following month or the business day before if the 7th falls on a weekend or public holiday; EMP501 interim season 1 September to 31 October and annual season 1 April to 31 May.

Filing-season closing dates for the 2026 year (23 October 2026 non-provisional, 22 January 2027 provisional) are from SARS Government Gazette No. 54598, Notice 7422, 30 April 2026, as reported by Accounter — https://accounter.co.za/tools/tax-calendar. Confirm on sars.gov.za each year.

**[Official] SARS — Guide to Provisional Tax**
https://www.sars.gov.za/guide-to-provisional-tax/
Supports: two provisional payments per year, the optional third top-up payment, and that a nil IRP6 can still be submitted.

**[Official] SARS — Tax Invoice Checklist**
https://www.sars.gov.za/wp-content/uploads/Docs/Government/Tax-Invoice-Checklist-Version-2-29032016.pdf
Supports: the seven criteria for a full tax invoice above R5,000 and the five criteria for an abridged tax invoice between R50 and R5,000.

**[Official] SARS — VAT 420 Guide for Motor Dealers**
https://www.sars.gov.za/wp-content/uploads/Ops/Guides/Legal-Pub-Guide-VAT420-VAT-420-Guide-for-Motor-Dealers.pdf
Supports: notional input tax calculation for motor dealers, the VAT264 requirement, and Binding General Ruling 12 on over-allowances. Read this in full before registering a dealership for VAT.

**[Official] SARS — VAT Connect Issue 20, October 2025**
https://www.sars.gov.za/businesses-and-employers/my-business-and-tax/vat-connect-issue-20-october-2025/
Supports: the worked notional input tax example (R115,000 purchase, R15,000 notional input, R3,000 VAT on a R20,000 margin).

**[Official] SARS — VAT 264 declaration for the supply of second-hand goods**
https://www.sars.gov.za/wp-content/uploads/Docs/VAT/VAT-264-declaration-for-the-supply-of-second-hand-goods-external-form.pdf
Supports: the declaration form required from both buyer and seller for a notional input tax claim.

Secondary, used to cross-check the 2026/2027 tables:
- Xero ZA, SARS Tax Tables 2026/2027 — https://www.xero.com/za/guides/sars-tax-tables-2026/ — tax thresholds of R99,000 (under 65), R153,250 (65 to 74), R171,300 (75+); retirement contribution cap of R430,000.
- SAIT, Turnover Tax vs SBC — https://thesait.org.za/small-business-tax-in-south-africa-turnover-tax-vs-sbc/ — worked example of R800,000 turnover producing R2,000 turnover tax.
- Cliffe Dekker Hofmeyr, 2026 Budget summary VAT — https://www.cliffedekkerhofmeyr.com/en/news/publications/2026/South-Africa/Tax-Exchange-Control/Tax-and-Exchange-Control-25-February-2026-Budget-summary-VAT

Note on a discrepancy found: two secondary sources gave different primary rebate figures (R17,235 and R17,820). The R99,000 threshold at an 18% first bracket implies R17,820. This toolkit quotes the threshold rather than the rebate for that reason. Confirm on the SARS tax rates page before relying on either.

---

### Company registration

**[Official] BizPortal (CIPC) — Company registration**
https://www.bizportal.gov.za/company_reg.aspx
Supports: R125 private company registration, R50 for one to four proposed names, automatic tax registration, registration-number-as-name option.

**[Official] CIPC**
https://www.cipc.co.za/
Supports: registration fee range of R125 to R475 depending on company type.

Secondary:
- Govchain, How to register a company directly with CIPC — https://www.govchain.co.za/blog/how-to-register-a-company-directly-with-cipc — the R175 total, the CoR 14.3 and MOI arriving by email, and the Home Affairs ID check failing on a single wrong digit.
- Smartbook, cost to register a company — https://www.smartbookie.co.za/blog/cost-to-register-a-company-south-africa — the realistic first-year and ongoing compliance cost estimates. Treat these as one firm's estimate, not a fixed figure.

---

### Running a Pty Ltd: annual returns, financial statements, directors

**[Official] CIPC — Annual Returns filing system**
https://annualreturns.cipc.co.za
Where annual returns are filed. You need a CIPC customer code.

**[Official] CIPC — Financial statements and independent reviews**
https://www.cipc.co.za/?page_id=11891
Supports: which companies require an audit, which require an independent review, and the role of the Public Interest Score and of internal versus independent compilation.

**[Official] SAICA — Independent Reviews**
https://www.saica.org.za/resources/audit-and-assurance/technical-resource/video-and-recording/other-assurance-and-related-services/independent-reviews/
Supports: independent reviews are not required for owner-managed profit companies, and who may perform a review at each PIS level.

Underlying law: Companies Act 71 of 2008 — s4 (solvency and liquidity test), s22 (reckless trading), s32 (company name and registration number on documents), s33 (annual returns), s76 (director duties), s77 (director liability), s218(2) (liability for contraventions). Consumer Protection Act 68 of 2008 — s79 and s80 (business names; the s80 register has not been brought into operation). Income Tax Act 58 of 1962 — s22 (trading stock brought into account at year end), s12E (small business corporations, including the restriction on holding shares in another company). Companies Regulations 2011 — regulation 26 (Public Interest Score), regulation 28 and 29 (audit and independent review), table CR 2B (annual return fees).

Secondary, for fees, deadlines and deregistration mechanics:
- Govchain, Company annual returns — https://www.govchain.co.za/services/company-annual-returns — the 30-business-day window from the incorporation anniversary, and that fees are legislated under table CR 2B.
- Admin Boss — https://www.adminboss.co.za/cipc-compliance-south-africa-annual-returns-beneficial-ownership-sars-deregistration/ — the R100 to R3,000 on-time fee range and R150 to R4,000 late range, and the SARS deregistration sequence and timeline estimate.
- OurPower — https://www.ourpower.co.za/tools/company-registration/annual-return-cipc-explained — the beneficial ownership hard stop since 1 July 2024, the two-year deregistration trigger, and the difference in cost between AR reinstatement and final-deregistration reinstatement.
- ThriveCFO — https://thrivecfo.co.za/cipc-annual-returns-avoid-deregistration-2026/ — consequences of deregistration including bona vacantia, and form CoR 40.5 reinstatement.
- ClearComply, Public Interest Score — https://www.clearcomply.co.za/blog/public-interest-score-south-africa — the owner-managed boundary, and why one non-director shareholder changes the obligation.
- CFO360, PIS calculator — https://cfo360.co.za/public-interest-score-calculator/ — how the points are counted and the under-100 owner-managed exemption.
- ElyForma — https://www.elyforma.com/blog/company-annual-returns-guide-2026 — the Financial Accountability Supplement alternative to statements.

Note on the 800,000 deregistration figure: this comes from a secondary source describing CIPC action begun in January 2025. Treat it as an indication of scale, not a precise official statistic.

---

### Paying yourself from a company

**[Official] SARS — Comprehensive Guide to Dividends Tax**
https://www.sars.gov.za/wp-content/uploads/Ops/Guides/LAPD-IT-G19-Comprehensive-Guide-to-Dividends-Tax.pdf
Supports: the section 64E(4) deemed dividend provisions, including the nature and amount of the deemed dividend and its deemed date of payment. Read this before acting on any debit loan account.

**[Official] SARS — Budget 2026 FAQ** (as above)
Supports: the official interest rate for the fringe benefit on interest-free or low-interest loans, 7.75% per annum from 1 December 2025.

Underlying law: Income Tax Act 58 of 1962 — s64E(4) (deemed dividends on low-interest loans to shareholders), the Seventh Schedule (fringe benefits), paragraph 11C of the Fourth Schedule (deemed remuneration for private company directors). Companies Act 71 of 2008 — s4 and s46 (distributions and the solvency and liquidity test).

Case law: Cohen NO v Segal, Witwatersrand Local Division, judgment 28 April 1970 — a dividend may generally only be declared out of profits, not capital. Confirm the law report citation with an attorney before relying on it in correspondence.

Secondary:
- Polity / ENSafrica, Directors of private companies and PAYE — https://www.polity.org.za/article/directors-of-private-companies-are-no-longer-subject-to-paye---true-or-false-2018-09-25 — confirms paragraph 11C was repealed with effect from 1 March 2017, and that section 7B governs the timing of variable remuneration.
- SimplePay, Deemed Remuneration — https://www.simplepay.co.za/help/payroll-concepts/deemed-remuneration/ — confirms deemed remuneration should no longer be used from 1 March 2017 and that directors are taxed on actual remuneration.
- **Not used:** TaxTim's article on a director's PAYE and UIF deductions, and PATC's equivalent, both still describe the repealed deemed remuneration formula as current. They were checked and rejected. This is a good example of why the official position matters more than a well-ranked article.
- The Tax Faculty, Dividends interest on loan — https://taxfaculty.ac.za/news/read/dividends-interest-on-loan — that the capital of a debit loan is not itself a deemed dividend, only the interest shortfall.
- MMS Cloud Accounting — https://www.mmscloudaccounting.co.za/on-the-right-side-of-directors-loan-accounts/ — the three ways to clear a debit loan account before year end.
- The Beancounter — https://thebeancounter.co.za/dear-director-hows-your-loan-account-looking/ — practical timing, and why waiting for the financial statements is too late.
- Govchain — https://www.govchain.co.za/blog/the-best-way-to-pay-yourself-as-a-company-owner — the risk when a loan is written off.

**Two conflicts recorded, both flagged in the toolkit text:**

1. *Scope of the deemed dividend.* Some sources state the deemed dividend is 20% of the entire debit loan balance. The Tax Faculty and the wording of s64E(4) support the narrower position that only the foregone interest is deemed a dividend. The toolkit takes the narrower position and says so.

2. *UIF for a sole director.* ClearComply and eqeight indicate UIF applies to a director paid through payroll even as the only employee. Other sources indicate UIF and SDL do not apply where the director receives director's fees and is not an employee under an employment contract. The toolkit presents both and advises getting advice rather than picking one.

- The Citizen — https://www.citizen.co.za/business/personal-finance/even-old-car-cannot-be-bought-voetstoots-used-car-dealer/ — Consumer Tribunal ruling that voetstoots does not apply to CPA transactions, and CPA s2(10) and s56(4).
- Moonstone — https://www.moonstone.co.za/ruling-confirms-consumers-may-go-straight-to-court-under-the-cpa/ — the December 2025 Western Cape High Court decision requiring a R175,000 refund, and that buyers need not exhaust ombud processes first.

https://www.justice.gov.za/scc/scc.htm

### Business licensing

**Business Act 71 of 1991.** Administered by your local municipality, not by national government. There is no single national list of licensable businesses, because municipalities apply the Act through their own by-laws.

Secondary:
- SME South Africa — https://smesouthafrica.co.za/sme-guides/business-licences-in-south-africa/
- Yebo Business — https://www.yebobusiness.co.za/business-licenses-required-south-africa

The only reliable source for your situation is your municipal Business Licensing office.

### Working from home, payments and personal safety

**[Official] South African Reserve Bank — banknote security features**
The SARB's "look, feel, tilt" method is published on www.resbank.co.za and in the SARB Currency App. The toolkit's description of the raised print, the visual-aid lines, the security thread, the watermark and the colour-changing ink is drawn from SARB material as reported by:
- IOL / The Mercury — https://iol.co.za/mercury/news/2022-11-02-how-to-spot-counterfeit-money-warning-issued-after-fake-r200-notes-were-used-at-a-durban-north-restaurant/
- The Citizen (local titles) — https://www.citizen.co.za/brakpan-herald/news-headlines/2023/03/13/know-your-money/ and https://www.citizen.co.za/south-coast-herald/news-headlines/local-news/2023/04/12/take-note-of-banknote-security-features/
Supports: that counterfeit notes cannot be exchanged and must be reported to SAPS; that the SARB alone may issue currency under section 14 of the SARB Act.

**[Official] SABRIC — South African Banking Risk Information Centre**
https://www.sabric.co.za
Supports: fraud reporting. The 2024 figures (digital banking fraud incidents up 86%, losses up 74% to R1.888 billion) are from SABRIC's 2024 Annual Crime Statistics as reported by Zapper — https://www.zapper.com/2026/07/28/how-to-protect-your-business-from-payment-fraud/

**PayShap limits**
- TechCentral — https://techcentral.co.za/payshap-payment-limit-raised-to-r50-000/254663/ — BankservAfrica raised the system limit from R3,000 to R50,000.
- Solmate — https://solmate.co.za/blog/payshap-instant-payments-south-africa/ — that banks set their own lower per-transaction and daily limits, and that payments clear in seconds and can go to a normal account number.
- Netcash — https://netcash.co.za/blog/how-businesses-can-use-payshap-for-payroll-creditor-payments/ — per-bank limit examples. Bank limits change; check your own app.

**Fake proof of payment and marketplace scams**
- EWN, Gumtree flags fake payment scam — https://www.ewn.co.za/2026/07/10/gumtree-flags-fake-payment-scam-targeting-sellers-on-online-marketplace — the case of a fake POP with an Uber sent to collect, from an account two days old.
- Cape Argus — https://capeargus.co.za/personal-finance/2026-03-07-the-message-looks-real-it-isnt/ — the SAPS-described pattern of fake proof of purchase, fake ID, and a driver sent to collect; overpayment scams.
- Flip the Market — https://flipthemarket.co.za/side-hustle-payments-and-avoiding-chargebacks-scams-and-proof-of-payment-bullsh-t/ — the "reflected, not pending" rule, the bank-reversal voice note, phishing links.
- Bizcommunity / Gumtree — https://www.bizcommunity.com/Article/196/357/129785.html — the cheque deposit and cancel scam.

**Meeting buyers and test drives**
- Tracker South Africa — https://www.tracker.co.za/news/news-room/test-drive-robbery — licence copy, keys handed over only once seated, geotag removal, not meeting at home, trusting instinct.
- Cars.co.za — https://www.cars.co.za/motoring-news/sell-your-used-car-to-a-private-buyer/73097/ and https://www.cars.co.za/motoring-news/selling-your-car-in-south-africa/251983/ — public place, bring someone, tell someone, licence check, key-swap warning, no solo test drives.
- MotorHappy — https://www.motorhappy.co.za/motorhappyblog/car-tips/selling-a-car-privately-in-south-africa — petrol station or police station as meeting places; cash handled inside a bank branch; no keys or documents until payment clears.

Note: South Africa has no formal national "safe exchange zone" programme at police stations. The suggestion to use police station parking is common practice, not an official scheme. Ask your local station.

**Home-based business zoning**
- Ashersons Attorneys — https://www.ashersons.co.za/articles/home-businesses-is-yours-legal/ — micro-business and home enterprise categories, and when consent or rezoning is needed.
- Yebo Business — https://www.yebobusiness.co.za/how-to-start-business-from-home-south-africa/ — the usual home enterprise conditions, including no visible storage of goods or vehicles, and which activities need consent use.
- FindAnAttorney — https://www.findanattorney.co.za/content_working-from-home-in-sectional-title-schemes — the quarter-of-floor-area rule of thumb and the enforceability of body corporate conduct rules.
- LookSee (Standard Bank) — https://www.looksee.co.za/looksee/home/knowledge-hub/articles/the-legalities-of-running-a-business-from-home — checking the title deed and zoning certificate; sectional title consent.
- Leapfrog — https://leapfrog.co.za/news/the-home-business-rulebook-zoning-insurance-and-municipal-compliance/ — household insurance may not cover business equipment or visitors.

Zoning rules are set by each municipality. The only reliable source for your property is your municipal town planning office.

### You are the business: UIF, COIDA, courts and continuity

**UIF and the self-employed.** Unemployment Insurance Contributions Act 2002, section 1 — "employee" means a natural person receiving remuneration for services rendered, excluding an independent contractor. The Tax Faculty applies this directly: https://taxfaculty.ac.za/faq/general_faqs/solution/1170 — a sole proprietor cannot contribute to the UIF because a sole proprietor cannot be an employee who becomes unemployed.

**COIDA and working directors.** The Act's definition of employee includes a director or member of a body corporate who has entered into a contract of service, in so far as they act within the scope of that employment, and excludes shareholders or silent partners who only receive dividends or profit share. See https://www.eohcb.co.za/post/coida-who-must-register-for-coida and https://www.mdacc.co.za/do-i-need-to-register-for-workmens-compensation/. That a working director paid a monthly salary must be registered and submit a Return of Earnings even as the only employee is from ClearComply's 2026 guide: https://www.clearcomply.co.za/blog/coida-compensation-fund-south-africa. Sole proprietors and partners are not employees under COIDA: https://labourguide.co.za/coid/compensation-fund-registrations/compensation-fund-registration

**Small Claims Court, who may sue.** Small Claims Courts Act 61 of 1984, section 7(1) — only a natural person may institute an action, and a juristic person may become a party only as defendant. Consolidated text: https://www.saflii.org/za/legis/consol_act/scca1984214/. Confirmed by Legal Aid South Africa (https://legal-aid.co.za/2018/09/26/instituting-action-in-the-small-claims-court-guide/), Werksmans Attorneys (https://werksmans.com/a-brief-overview-of-the-small-claims-court-2026-update/) and the Department of Justice (https://www.justice.gov.za/scc/scc_info.htm). A nominated director or officer may appear for a juristic person that is a defendant. The three-year prescription period is from the Werksmans overview.

**Death of a sole director and shareholder.** Companies Act 71 of 2008 — a person ceases to be a director on death; directors are appointed by shareholders. Administration of Estates Act 66 of 1965, section 13(1) — a deceased estate must be reported to the Master of the High Court within 14 days. South African commentary on the resulting paralysis, the dependence on the MOI, and the Saga Wines matter: Al Baraka Bank legal services (https://www.albaraka.co.za/blogs/legalease-3-2023/business-owners-beware-your-death-could-result-in-the-closure-of-your-business), Shandu Attorneys (https://shanduattorneys.co.za/2019/11/27/business-continuity-risk-death-of-a-companys-sole-shareholder-and-sole-director/), FindAnAttorney (https://www.findanattorney.co.za/content_company-law-deceased-estate) and Iota (https://www.iota.co.za/news-1/business-continuity-of-sole-shareholder-and-director-dying), which gives the two-weeks-to-three-months estimate for appointing an executor.

Caution: several widely-read articles on this topic are written for the UK Model Articles or Australia's section 201F. **Neither applies in South Africa.** The position here depends on your own MOI. Ask an attorney.

### Colour research and signage materials

**Colour psychology.** The toolkit deliberately avoids colour-meaning charts. The position is based on:

- Elliot, A. J. and Maier, M. A., "Color Psychology: Effects of Perceiving Color on Psychological Functioning in Humans", *Annual Review of Psychology* (2014) — no universal meaning for any colour; effects are context-dependent and vary with saturation, brightness, product category and culture.
- Labrecque, L. I. and Milne, G. R., "Exciting Red and Competent Blue: The Importance of Color in Marketing", *Journal of the Academy of Marketing Science* (2012) — consistency between brand colour and perceived brand personality predicts preference better than the colour itself.
- The frequently quoted claim that 62 to 90% of a snap product judgment comes from colour traces to a 2006 literature review by Satyendra Singh, not to a controlled buying experiment. The toolkit does not use it.

Summaries consulted: https://www.deepmarketing.it/en/blog/color-science-marketing-evidence-2026 and https://howbrandsarebuilt.com/branding-color-psychology/. The underlying papers were not read in full; if a decision turns on this, read Elliot and Maier directly.

**Signage vinyl and South African UV.**

- Sytech Supplies (South Africa) — https://www.sytech.co.za/2025/10/14/understanding-common-vinyl-failures-in-south-africas-climate-and-how-to-prevent-them/ — that South Africa sits in a Zone 3 UV climate; that vertical vinyl lasting up to ten years in Europe can fade in three years locally; that horizontal installations can lose 50 to 70% of expected lifespan. This is a supplier's blog, not an independent test. Treat the figures as an order of magnitude.
- Cast versus calendered lifespans (cast roughly 5 to 10 years outdoors, calendered roughly 1 to 5) are consistent across several independent trade sources, including https://www.craftsmenind.com/blog/fleet-graphics-lifespan and https://swiftsignquote.com/blog/cast-vs-calendered-vinyl. Local conditions and installation quality move these figures substantially.

Get your own sign maker to confirm what they are quoting before you order.

### Branding tools referenced

- WebAIM contrast checker — https://webaim.org/resources/contrastchecker/ — used to verify text and background colour pairs against WCAG AA. The toolkit tells readers to do this themselves because language models cannot reliably compute contrast ratios.
- Google Fonts — https://fonts.google.com — fonts licensed under the SIL Open Font Licence, which permits commercial use. Check the licence page of any font before use.
- Inkscape — https://inkscape.org — free vector editor. The toolkit's recommended tool for producing the final logo file.
- CIPC trade mark search — via the CIPC eServices portal at www.cipc.co.za. The toolkit tells readers to search this themselves before committing to a name.

The position that AI-generated images may be difficult to own or register as a trade mark is unsettled law and is presented in the toolkit as a risk to manage, not a rule.

### Where to go when this toolkit is not enough

| Question | Who to ask |
|---|---|
| Tax on my specific numbers | A SARS-registered tax practitioner. Verify their registration on eFiling. |
| Whether my business needs a licence | Your municipal Business Licensing office |
| Food premises | Your municipal Environmental Health Practitioner |
| A contract worth more than a month of profit | An attorney |
| Vehicle registration specifics | Your registering authority, in person |
| Second-hand goods dealer registration | The Designated Police Officer at your local SAPS station |
| A consumer complaint against you | The relevant ombud, before it reaches a tribunal |

One hour of professional advice costs less than one penalty. Budget for it.
